from collections import deque

def min_moves(n):
    # n odd, start pattern tails on odd positions (1-indexed)
    init = tuple(1 if i%2==0 else 0 for i in range(n))  # 0-indexed: i even => position i+1 odd => tail=1
    target = tuple([0]*n)
    # BFS over (pos, state) where pos is current index (0..n-1)
    # First move can be any position, so we initialize queue with all possible first moves.
    visited = set()
    q = deque()
    for start in range(n):
        # flip start coin
        state = list(init)
        state[start] ^= 1
        tup = (start, tuple(state))
        visited.add(tup)
        q.append((tup, 1))  # moves count =1
    while q:
        (pos, state), d = q.popleft()
        if state == target:
            return d
        # next moves to adjacent positions
        for nxt in (pos-1, pos+1):
            if 0 <= nxt < n:
                new_state = list(state)
                new_state[nxt] ^= 1
                tup = (nxt, tuple(new_state))
                if tup not in visited:
                    visited.add(tup)
                    q.append((tup, d+1))
    return None

for n in range(1,12,2):
    print(n, min_moves(n))

from collections import deque

def min_moves_bitmask(n):
    init_mask = 0
    for i in range(n):
        if i%2==0: # odd position tails
            init_mask |= (1<<i)
    target_mask = 0
    visited = set()
    q = deque()
    for start in range(n):
        mask = init_mask ^ (1<<start)  # flip start
        state = (start, mask)
        visited.add(state)
        q.append((state, 1))
    while q:
        (pos, mask), d = q.popleft()
        if mask == target_mask:
            return d
        for nxt in (pos-1, pos+1):
            if 0 <= nxt < n:
                new_mask = mask ^ (1<<nxt)
                st = (nxt, new_mask)
                if st not in visited:
                    visited.add(st)
                    q.append((st, d+1))
    return None

def test_upto(N):
    for n in range(1,N+1,2):
        mm = min_moves_bitmask(n)
        if n==1:
            formula = 1
        elif n%4==1:
            formula = 2*n-3
        else:
            formula = 2*n-2
        print(n, mm, formula, mm==formula)

test_upto(21)

def get_path(n):
    init_mask = 0
    for i in range(n):
        if i%2==0:
            init_mask |= (1<<i)
    target_mask = 0
    from collections import deque
    visited = {}
    q = deque()
    for start in range(n):
        mask = init_mask ^ (1<<start)
        state = (start, mask)
        visited[state] = None  # predecessor
        q.append((state, 1))
    while q:
        (pos, mask), d = q.popleft()
        if mask == target_mask:
            # reconstruct path
            path = []
            cur = (pos, mask)
            while cur is not None:
                path.append(cur[0])
                cur = visited[cur]
            path = path[::-1]  # start to end
            return path
        for nxt in (pos-1, pos+1):
            if 0 <= nxt < n:
                new_mask = mask ^ (1<<nxt)
                st = (nxt, new_mask)
                if st not in visited:
                    visited[st] = (pos, mask)
                    q.append((st, d+1))
    return None

print(get_path(5))

    init_mask = 0
    for i in range(n):
        if i%2==0:
            init_mask |= (1<<i)
    target_mask = 0
    from collections import deque
    visited = {}
    q = deque()
    for start in range(n):
        mask = init_mask ^ (1<<start)
        state = (start, mask)
        visited[state] = None  # predecessor
        q.append((state, 1))
    while q:
        (pos, mask), d = q.popleft()
        if mask == target_mask:
            # reconstruct path
            path = []
            cur = (pos, mask)
            while cur is not None:
                path.append(cur[0])
                cur = visited[cur]
            path = path[::-1]  # start to end
            return path
        for nxt in (pos-1, pos+1):
            if 0 <= nxt < n:
                new_mask = mask ^ (1<<nxt)
                st = (nxt, new_mask)
                if st not in visited:
                    visited[st] = (pos, mask)
                    q.append((st, d+1))

[CODE] (turn 8):
def simulate_path(n, path):
    # path list of positions (0-index)
    state = [1 if i%2==0 else 0 for i in range(n)]  # 1 tail,0 head
    for pos in path:
        state[pos] ^= 1
    return state

def check_path(n, path):
    final = simulate_path(n, path)
    return all(v==0 for v in final)

path9 = get_path(9)
print(path9, len(path9))
print(check_path(9, path9))

[CODE] (turn 1):
from collections import deque

def min_moves(n):
    # n odd
    # initial state: pattern T H T H ... (odd T)
    # Represent state as tuple of bits: 0=heads, 1=tails? Let's use 1 for T (needs odd flips)
    # initial: odd positions 1, even 0.
    init = tuple(1 if i%2==1 else 0 for i in range(1,n+1))
    target = tuple(0 for _ in range(n))
    # BFS over (state, last_pos) where last_pos is index of last flipped coin (0-indexed)
    # first move can be any coin: we treat last_pos = None initially.
    from functools import lru_cache
    start = (init, None)
    q = deque()
    q.append((init, None, 0))
    seen = set()
    seen.add((init, None))
    while q:
        state, last, dist = q.popleft()
        if state == target:
            return dist
        # possible moves:
        if last is None:
            candidates = range(n)
        else:
            candidates = []
            if last-1 >= 0:
                candidates.append(last-1)
            if last+1 < n:
                candidates.append(last+1)
        for i in candidates:
            new_state = list(state)
            new_state[i] ^= 1  # flip
            new_state = tuple(new_state)
            new_last = i
            key = (new_state, new_last)
            if key not in seen:
                seen.add(key)
                q.append((new_state, new_last, dist+1))
    return None

for n in [1,3,5,7,9,11,13]:
    print(n, min_moves(n))

from collections import deque

def min_moves(n):
    # n odd
    # initial state: pattern T H T H ... (odd T)
    # Represent state as tuple of bits: 0=heads, 1=tails? Let's use 1 for T (needs odd flips)
    # initial: odd positions 1, even 0.
    init = tuple(1 if i%2==1 else 0 for i in range(1,n+1))
    target = tuple(0 for _ in range(n))
    # BFS over (state, last_pos) where last_pos is index of last flipped coin (0-indexed)
    # first move can be any coin: we treat last_pos = None initially.
    from functools import lru_cache
    start = (init, None)
    q = deque()
    q.append((init, None, 0))
    seen = set()
    seen.add((init, None))
    while q:
        state, last, dist = q.popleft()
        if state == target:
            return dist
        # possible moves:
        if last is None:
            candidates = range(n)
        else:
            candidates = []
            if last-1 >= 0:
                candidates.append(last-1)
            if last+1 < n:
                candidates.append(last+1)
        for i in candidates:
            new_state = list(state)
            new_state[i] ^= 1  # flip
            new_state = tuple(new_state)
            new_last = i
            key = (new_state, new_last)
            if key not in seen:
                seen.add(key)
                q.append((new_state, new_last, dist+1))
    return None

for n in [1,3,5,7,9,11,13]:

def min_moves_limit(n, limit=100000):
    # BFS with pruning maybe large but n up to 31 manageable
    from collections import deque
    init = tuple(1 if i%2==1 else 0 for i in range(1,n+1))
    target = tuple(0 for _ in range(n))
    start = (init, None)
    q = deque()
    q.append((init, None, 0))
    seen = set()
    seen.add((init, None))
    while q:
        state, last, dist = q.popleft()
        if state == target:
            return dist
        if dist >= limit:
            return None
        if last is None:
            candidates = range(n)
        else:
            candidates = []
            if last-1 >= 0:
                candidates.append(last-1)
            if last+1 < n:
                candidates.append(last+1)
        for i in candidates:
            new_state = list(state)
            new_state[i] ^= 1
            new_state = tuple(new_state)
            key = (new_state, i)
            if key not in seen:
                seen.add(key)
                q.append((new_state, i, dist+1))
    return None

results = {}
for n in range(1,21,2):
    results[n] = min_moves_limit(n, limit=200)
print(results)

def min_moves_limit(n, limit=100000):
    # BFS with pruning maybe large but n up to 31 manageable
    from collections import deque
    init = tuple(1 if i%2==1 else 0 for i in range(1,n+1))
    target = tuple(0 for _ in range(n))
    start = (init, None)
    q = deque()
    q.append((init, None, 0))
    seen = set()
    seen.add((init, None))
    while q:
        state, last, dist = q.popleft()
        if state == target:
            return dist
        if dist >= limit:
            return None
        if last is None:
            candidates = range(n)
        else:
            candidates = []
            if last-1 >= 0:
                candidates.append(last-1)
            if last+1 < n:
                candidates.append(last+1)
        for i in candidates:
            new_state = list(state)
            new_state[i] ^= 1
            new_state = tuple(new_state)
            key = (new_state, i)
            if key not in seen:
                seen.add(key)
                q.append((new_state, i, dist+1))
    return None

results = {}
for n in range(1,21,2):
    results[n] = min_moves_limit(n, limit=200)
print(results)

def moves_formula(n):
    if n==1:
        return 1
    return (3*n -1)//2 + 2* ((n-3)//4)
for n in range(1,21,2):
    print(n, min_moves_limit(n), moves_formula(n))

def min_moves_bfs(n, max_steps=200):
    from collections import deque
    init = tuple(1 if i%2==1 else 0 for i in range(1,n+1))
    target = tuple(0 for _ in range(n))
    q = deque()
    q.append((init, None, 0))
    seen = set()
    seen.add((init, None))
    while q:
        state, last, dist = q.popleft()
        if state == target:
            return dist
        if dist > max_steps:
            continue
        if last is None:
            candidates = range(n)
        else:
            candidates = []
            if last-1 >= 0:
                candidates.append(last-1)
            if last+1 < n:
                candidates.append(last+1)
        for i in candidates:
            new_state = list(state)
            new_state[i] ^= 1
            new_state = tuple(new_state)
            key = (new_state, i)
            if key not in seen:
                seen.add(key)
                q.append((new_state, i, dist+1))
    return None

for n in range(21,27,2):
    print(n, min_moves_bfs(n, max_steps=200))

def min_moves_bfs(n, max_steps=200):
    from collections import deque
    init = tuple(1 if i%2==1 else 0 for i in range(1,n+1))
    target = tuple(0 for _ in range(n))
    q = deque()
    q.append((init, None, 0))
    seen = set()
    seen.add((init, None))
    while q:
        state, last, dist = q.popleft()
        if state == target:
            return dist
        if dist > max_steps:
            continue
        if last is None:
            candidates = range(n)
        else:
            candidates = []
            if last-1 >= 0:
                candidates.append(last-1)
            if last+1 < n:
                candidates.append(last+1)
        for i in candidates:
            new_state = list(state)
            new_state[i] ^= 1
            new_state = tuple(new_state)
            key = (new_state, i)
            if key not in seen:
                seen.add(key)
                q.append((new_state, i, dist+1))
    return None

for n in range(21,27,2):
    print(n, min_moves_bfs(n, max_steps=200))

[CODE] (turn 5):
def find_path(n, target_len=None):
    from collections import deque
    init = tuple(1 if i%2==1 else 0 for i in range(1,n+1))
    target = tuple(0 for _ in range(n))
    q = deque()
    q.append((init, None, []))
    seen = set()
    seen.add((init, None))
    while q:
        state, last, path = q.popleft()
        if state == target:
            return path
        if target_len is not None and len(path) >= target_len:
            continue
        if last is None:
            candidates = range(n)
        else:
            candidates = []
            if last-1 >= 0:
                candidates.append(last-1)
            if last+1 < n:
                candidates.append(last+1)
        for i in candidates:
            new_state = list(state)
            new_state[i] ^= 1
            new_state = tuple(new_state)
            key = (new_state, i)
            if key not in seen:
                seen.add(key)
                q.append((new_state, i, path+[i+1]))  # store 1-indexed positions
    return None

def find_path(n, target_len=None):
    from collections import deque
    init = tuple(1 if i%2==1 else 0 for i in range(1,n+1))
    target = tuple(0 for _ in range(n))
    q = deque()
    q.append((init, None, []))
    seen = set()
    seen.add((init, None))
    while q:
        state, last, path = q.popleft()
        if state == target:
            return path
        if target_len is not None and len(path) >= target_len:
            continue
        if last is None:
            candidates = range(n)
        else:
            candidates = []
            if last-1 >= 0:
                candidates.append(last-1)
            if last+1 < n:
                candidates.append(last+1)
        for i in candidates:
            new_state = list(state)
            new_state[i] ^= 1
            new_state = tuple(new_state)
            key = (new_state, i)
            if key not in seen:
                seen.add(key)
                q.append((new_state, i, path+[i+1]))  # store 1-indexed positions
    return None

[CODE] (turn 9):
def construct_path(n):
    path = []
    if n==1:
        return [1]
    if n%4==3:
        q = (n-3)//4
        for j in range(q):
            a = 4*j+1
            path += [a, a+1, a+2, a+1, a+2, a+3, a+2, a+3]
        # tail length 3
        a = 4*q+1
        path += [a, a+1, a+2, a+1]
    else: # n%4==1
        if n==5:
            # tail only
            a = 1
            path += [a+1, a, a+1, a+2, a+3, a+4, a+3]
            return path
        q = (n-5)//4
        for j in range(q):
            a = 4*j+1
            path += [a, a+1, a+2, a+1, a+2, a+3, a+2, a+3]
        a = 4*q+1
        # tail length 5 pattern
        path += [a+1, a, a+1, a+2, a+3, a+4, a+3]
    return path

def verify_path(path, n):
    # simulate flips
    state = [1 if i%2==1 else 0 for i in range(1,n+1)]
    for pos in path:
        state[pos-1] ^= 1
    return all(s==0 for s in state)

for n in range(1,25,2):
    p = construct_path(n)
    ok = verify_path(p, n)
    print(n, len(p), ok)

def construct_path(n):
    path = []
    if n==1:
        return [1]
    if n%4==3:
        q = (n-3)//4
        for j in range(q):
            a = 4*j+1
            path += [a, a+1, a+2, a+1, a+2, a+3, a+2, a+3]
        # tail length 3
        a = 4*q+1
        path += [a, a+1, a+2, a+1]
    else: # n%4==1
        if n==5:
            # tail only
            a = 1
            path += [a+1, a, a+1, a+2, a+3, a+4, a+3]
            return path
        q = (n-5)//4
        for j in range(q):
            a = 4*j+1
            path += [a, a+1, a+2, a+1, a+2, a+3, a+2, a+3]
        a = 4*q+1
        # tail length 5 pattern
        path += [a+1, a, a+1, a+2, a+3, a+4, a+3]
    return path

def verify_path(path, n):
    # simulate flips
    state = [1 if i%2==1 else 0 for i in range(1,n+1)]
    for pos in path:
        state[pos-1] ^= 1
    return all(s==0 for s in state)

for n in range(1,25,2):
    p = construct_path(n)
    ok = verify_path(p, n)
    print(n, len(p), ok)

from collections import deque

def min_moves(N):
    # N odd, initial pattern alternating T (odd) need odd flips, H (even) need even flips
    # Represent required parity: 1 for odd positions, 0 for even.
    req = [i%2 for i in range(1,N+1)]  # 1 for odd, 0 for even
    # BFS over state: (position, parity_vector) where parity_vector is tuple of current parity (0/1) of flips at each position modulo 2.
    # Start: we haven't flipped any coin yet, but we will make first flip at chosen start position.
    # We'll treat BFS from all possible start positions.
    start_states = []
    for start in range(N):
        # after first flip at start
        parity = [0]*N
        parity[start] ^= 1
        start_states.append((start, tuple(parity), 1))  # moves count =1
    # BFS
    visited = {}
    q = deque()
    for pos,par,steps in start_states:
        key=(pos,par)
        visited[key]=steps
        q.append((pos,par,steps))
    while q:
        pos,par,steps = q.popleft()
        # check goal
        if list(par)==req:
            return steps
        # expand moves to adjacent positions
        for nxt in [pos-1,pos+1]:
            if 0<=nxt<N:
                newpar = list(par)
                newpar[nxt] ^= 1
                newpar = tuple(newpar)
                key=(nxt,newpar)
                if key not in visited:
                    visited[key]=steps+1
                    q.append((nxt,newpar,steps+1))
    return None

for N in [3,5,7,9]:
    print(N, min_moves(N))

def get_path(N):
    req = [i%2 for i in range(1,N+1)]
    from collections import deque
    start_states = []
    for start in range(N):
        parity = [0]*N
        parity[start] ^= 1
        start_states.append((start, tuple(parity), [start]))
    visited = {}
    q = deque()
    for pos,par,path in start_states:
        key=(pos,par)
        visited[key]=len(path)
        q.append((pos,par,path))
    while q:
        pos,par,path = q.popleft()
        if list(par)==req:
            return path
        for nxt in [pos-1,pos+1]:
            if 0<=nxt<N:
                newpar = list(par)
                newpar[nxt] ^= 1
                newpar = tuple(newpar)
                key=(nxt,newpar)
                if key not in visited:
                    visited[key]=len(path)+1
                    q.append((nxt,newpar,path+[nxt]))
    return None

print(get_path(5))

[CODE] (turn 1):
from collections import deque

def min_moves(N):
    # initial coin states: 1 for T (needs odd flips), 0 for H (needs even flips)
    init = [(i%2) for i in range(N)]  # 0-indexed: i=0 leftmost T -> 1
    # Represent state as (pos, bitmask)
    # bitmask bits 0..N-1: 1 if coin currently T (needs odd flips), 0 if H
    init_mask = 0
    for i,b in enumerate(init):
        if b:
            init_mask |= (1<<i)
    goal_mask = 0
    # BFS
    visited = {}
    q = deque()
    # first move: flip any coin i
    for i in range(N):
        mask = init_mask ^ (1<<i)  # flip coin i
        state = (i, mask)
        visited[state] = 1  # moves count
        q.append(state)
    while q:
        pos, mask = q.popleft()
        moves = visited[(pos,mask)]
        if mask == goal_mask:
            return moves
        # next moves: flip adjacent coin
        for np in [pos-1, pos+1]:
            if 0 <= np < N:
                nmask = mask ^ (1<<np)
                nstate = (np, nmask)
                if nstate not in visited:
                    visited[nstate] = moves+1
                    q.append(nstate)
    return None

for N in range(1,13,2):  # odd N
    print(N, min_moves(N))

def min_moves_debug(N):
    init = [(i%2) for i in range(N)]
    init_mask = 0
    for i,b in enumerate(init):
        if b:
            init_mask |= (1<<i)
    print("init_mask", bin(init_mask))
    goal_mask = 0
    for i in range(N):
        mask = init_mask ^ (1<<i)
        print("flip", i, bin(mask))
        if mask == goal_mask:
            print("found")
            return 1
    return None

print(min_moves_debug(1))

[CODE] (turn 7):
def min_moves_dp(N):
    # returns minimal moves (L) for given N
    # N odd
    INF = 10**9
    best = INF
    # iterate start s, end t (0-index)
    for s in range(N):
        for t in range(N):
            # c_i = (i==s)+(i==t)
            # DP over i from 0 to N (vertices). Edge indices 0..N-2 correspond to m_i between i and i+1.
            # We'll keep possible values for previous m_{i-1} (for i=0, define m_{-1}=0 as no edge left of vertex 0)
            # Actually for vertex 0 (i=0), constraint uses m_{-1}=0 and m_0.
            # We'll DP storing minimal total sum up to edge i-1 given current m_{i-1} value.
            dp = {0:0}  # key: m_{i-1} value, value: sum of m up to i-2 (since none yet)
            for i in range(N):
                c = (1 if i==s else 0) + (1 if i==t else 0)
                p = 1 if (i%2==0) else 0  # odd index (1-indexed) corresponds to i even (0-index)
                # Determine required sum modulo 4:
                if p==1:  # odd vertex (i even)
                    target_mod = (2 - c) % 4
                else:  # even vertex (i odd)
                    target_mod = (-c) % 4
                new_dp = {}
                for prev_m, total in dp.items():
                    # need to choose current m_i (edge i) for i from 0..N-2; for i=N-1 there is no edge after, treat m_i = 0
                    # For i < N-1, we will set m_i variable; for i == N-1, we enforce m_{N-1}=0 (no edge after last vertex)
                    if i == N-1:
                        # No edge after last vertex, so current m_i = 0
                        cur_m = 0
                        if (prev_m + cur_m) % 4 == target_mod:
                            # total sum unchanged (no addition)
                            new_dp[cur_m] = min(new_dp.get(cur_m, INF), total)
                    else:
                        # choose cur_m in {0,1,2,3}
                        for cur_m in range(4):
                            if (prev_m + cur_m) % 4 == target_mod:
                                new_total = total + cur_m
                                # store for next iteration (prev_m becomes cur_m)
                                if cur_m not in new_dp or new_total < new_dp[cur_m]:
                                    new_dp[cur_m] = new_total
                dp = new_dp
                if not dp:
                    break
            else:
                # after loop, dp contains possible m_{N-1}=0 values (should be only key 0)
                # minimal total sum of m_i
                M = min(dp.values())
                L = M + 1
                if L < best:
                    best = L
    return best

for N in range(1,21,2):
    print(N, min_moves(N), min_moves_dp(N))

[CODE] (turn 7):
def min_moves_dp(N):
    # returns minimal moves (L) for given N
    # N odd
    INF = 10**9
    best = INF
    # iterate start s, end t (0-index)
    for s in range(N):
        for t in range(N):
            # c_i = (i==s)+(i==t)
            # DP over i from 0 to N (vertices). Edge indices 0..N-2 correspond to m_i between i and i+1.
            # We'll keep possible values for previous m_{i-1} (for i=0, define m_{-1}=0 as no edge left of vertex 0)
            # Actually for vertex 0 (i=0), constraint uses m_{-1}=0 and m_0.
            # We'll DP storing minimal total sum up to edge i-1 given current m_{i-1} value.
            dp = {0:0}  # key: m_{i-1} value, value: sum of m up to i-2 (since none yet)
            for i in range(N):
                c = (1 if i==s else 0) + (1 if i==t else 0)
                p = 1 if (i%2==0) else 0  # odd index (1-indexed) corresponds to i even (0-index)
                # Determine required sum modulo 4:
                if p==1:  # odd vertex (i even)
                    target_mod = (2 - c) % 4
                else:  # even vertex (i odd)
                    target_mod = (-c) % 4
                new_dp = {}
                for prev_m, total in dp.items():
                    # need to choose current m_i (edge i) for i from 0..N-2; for i=N-1 there is no edge after, treat m_i = 0
                    # For i < N-1, we will set m_i variable; for i == N-1, we enforce m_{N-1}=0 (no edge after last vertex)
                    if i == N-1:
                        # No edge after last vertex, so current m_i = 0
                        cur_m = 0
                        if (prev_m + cur_m) % 4 == target_mod:
                            # total sum unchanged (no addition)
                            new_dp[cur_m] = min(new_dp.get(cur_m, INF), total)
                    else:
                        # choose cur_m in {0,1,2,3}
                        for cur_m in range(4):
                            if (prev_m + cur_m) % 4 == target_mod:
                                new_total = total + cur_m
                                # store for next iteration (prev_m becomes cur_m)
                                if cur_m not in new_dp or new_total < new_dp[cur_m]:
                                    new_dp[cur_m] = new_total
                dp = new_dp
                if not dp:
                    break
            else:
                # after loop, dp contains possible m_{N-1}=0 values (should be only key 0)
                # minimal total sum of m_i
                M = min(dp.values())
                L = M + 1
                if L < best:
                    best = L
    return best

[CODE] (turn 8):
def min_moves_dp(N):
    INF = 10**9
    best = INF
    for s in range(N):
        for t in range(N):
            dp = {0:0}
            for i in range(N):
                c = (1 if i==s else 0) + (1 if i==t else 0)
                p = 1 if (i%2==0) else 0  # odd vertex if i even
                # target modulo condition:
                if p==1:
                    target_mod = (2 - c) % 4
                else:
                    target_mod = (-c) % 4
                new_dp = {}
                for prev_m, total in dp.items():
                    if i == N-1:
                        cur_m = 0
                        if (prev_m + cur_m) % 4 == target_mod:
                            # check inequality for odd vertex (i even) if needed
                            if p==1:
                                if prev_m + cur_m + c < 2:
                                    continue
                            # else even vertex no lower bound
                            new_total = total
                            # store final (cur_m=0) but not needed further
                            new_dp[cur_m] = min(new_dp.get(cur_m, INF), new_total)
                    else:
                        for cur_m in range(4):
                            if (prev_m + cur_m) % 4 != target_mod:
                                continue
                            # inequality for odd vertex
                            if p==1:
                                if prev_m + cur_m + c < 2:
                                    continue
                            new_total = total + cur_m
                            if cur_m not in new_dp or new_total < new_dp[cur_m]:
                                new_dp[cur_m] = new_total
                dp = new_dp
                if not dp:
                    break
            else:
                M = min(dp.values())
                L = M + 1
                if L < best:
                    best = L
    return best

for N in range(1,21,2):
    print(N, min_moves(N), min_moves_dp(N))

def min_moves_path(N):
    init_mask = 0
    for i in range(N):
        if i%2==0:
            init_mask |= (1<<i)
    goal_mask = 0
    from collections import deque
    visited = {}
    q = deque()
    for i in range(N):
        mask = init_mask ^ (1<<i)
        state = (i, mask)
        visited[state] = (1, None)  # moves, predecessor
        if mask == goal_mask:
            return 1, state
        q.append(state)
    while q:
        pos, mask = q.popleft()
        moves,_ = visited[(pos,mask)]
        if mask == goal_mask:
            return moves, (pos,mask)
        for np in (pos-1, pos+1):
            if 0 <= np < N:
                nmask = mask ^ (1<<np)
                nstate = (np, nmask)
                if nstate not in visited:
                    visited[nstate] = (moves+1, (pos,mask))
                    if nmask == goal_mask:
                        return moves+1, nstate
                    q.append(nstate)
    return None, None

moves, state = min_moves_path(5)
print("moves", moves)

    goal_mask = 0

    visited = {}

        state = (i, mask)
        visited[state] = (1, None)  # moves, predecessor
        if mask == goal_mask:
            return 1, state
        q.append(state)
    while q:
        pos, mask = q.popleft()
        moves,_ = visited[(pos,mask)]
        if mask == goal_mask:
            return moves, (pos,mask)
        for np in (pos-1, pos+1):
            if 0 <= np < N:
                nmask = mask ^ (1<<np)
                nstate = (np, nmask)
                if nstate not in visited:
                    visited[nstate] = (moves+1, (pos,mask))
                    if nmask == goal_mask:
                        return moves+1, nstate
                    q.append(nstate)
    return None, None

def min_moves_dp2(N):
    INF = 10**9
    best = INF
    for s in range(N):
        for t in range(N):
            dp = {0:0}
            for i in range(N):
                c = (1 if i==s else 0) + (1 if i==t else 0)
                p = 1 if (i%2==0) else 0
                if p==1:
                    target_mod = (2 - c) % 4
                else:
                    target_mod = (-c) % 4
                new_dp = {}
                for prev_m, total in dp.items():
                    if i == N-1:
                        cur_m = 0
                        if (prev_m + cur_m) % 4 != target_mod:
                            continue
                        if p==1 and prev_m + cur_m + c < 2:
                            continue
                        new_total = total
                        new_dp[cur_m] = min(new_dp.get(cur_m, INF), new_total)
                    else:
                        # require cur_m >=1 because edge must be traversed at least once
                        for cur_m in range(1,4):  # 1..3
                            if (prev_m + cur_m) % 4 != target_mod:
                                continue
                            if p==1 and prev_m + cur_m + c < 2:
                                continue
                            new_total = total + cur_m
                            if cur_m not in new_dp or new_total < new_dp[cur_m]:
                                new_dp[cur_m] = new_total
                dp = new_dp
                if not dp:
                    break
            else:
                M = min(dp.values())
                L = M + 1
                if L < best:
                    best = L
    return best

for N in range(1,21,2):
    print(N, min_moves(N), min_moves_dp2(N))

def extra_formula2(N):
    total = 0
    k = 2
    while k <= N:
        total += (N-1)//k
        k *= 2
    return total
for N in range(1,41,2):

def formula(N):
    if N==1:
        return 1
    if N%4==1:
        return 2*N - 3
    else:
        return 2*N - 2

[CODE] (turn 1):
def min_moves(N):
    # N odd, start pattern tails at odd positions (1-indexed)
    p = [ (i%2) for i in range(1,N+1) ]  # odd->1 (need odd visits), even->0
    # We'll brute force over start a, end b, base parity c (0/1) and compute minimal sum e_i (using 0/1 values)
    best = None
    best_info = None
    for a in range(1,N+1):
        for b in range(1,N+1):
            for c in [0,1]:
                # assign e_i values
                e = [0]*(N-1)
                if a==b:
                    # constant parity c
                    for i in range(N-1):
                        e[i]=c
                else:
                    # ensure a<b ordering for simplicity
                    if a<b:
                        for i in range(N-1):
                            if i < a-1:
                                e[i]=c
                            elif i >= a-1 and i < b-1:
                                e[i]=1-c
                            else:
                                e[i]=c
                    else:
                        # a>b
                        for i in range(N-1):
                            if i < b-1:
                                e[i]=c
                            elif i >= b-1 and i < a-1:
                                e[i]=1-c
                            else:
                                e[i]=c
                # compute v_i = (e_{i-1}+e_i + s_i + t_i)/2
                v = []
                for i in range(1,N+1):
                    e_left = e[i-2] if i-2 >=0 else 0
                    e_right = e[i-1] if i-1 < N-1 else 0
                    s = 1 if i==a else 0
                    t = 1 if i==b else 0
                    vi = (e_left + e_right + s + t)//2
                    # ensure integer division exact
                    if (e_left + e_right + s + t) % 2 !=0:
                        # invalid parity (should be even)
                        vi = None
                    v.append(vi)
                # check parity condition
                ok=True
                for i,vi in enumerate(v):
                    if vi is None:
                        ok=False
                        break
                    if vi%2 != p[i]:
                        ok=False
                        break
                if not ok:
                    continue
                moves = sum(v)
                if best is None or moves < best:
                    best = moves
                    best_info = (a,b,c,e,v)
    return best, best_info

print(min_moves(7))

[CODE] (turn 1):
def min_moves(N):
    # N odd, start pattern tails at odd positions (1-indexed)
    p = [ (i%2) for i in range(1,N+1) ]  # odd->1 (need odd visits), even->0
    # We'll brute force over start a, end b, base parity c (0/1) and compute minimal sum e_i (using 0/1 values)
    best = None
    best_info = None
    for a in range(1,N+1):
        for b in range(1,N+1):
            for c in [0,1]:
                # assign e_i values
                e = [0]*(N-1)
                if a==b:
                    # constant parity c
                    for i in range(N-1):
                        e[i]=c
                else:
                    # ensure a<b ordering for simplicity
                    if a<b:
                        for i in range(N-1):
                            if i < a-1:
                                e[i]=c
                            elif i >= a-1 and i < b-1:
                                e[i]=1-c
                            else:
                                e[i]=c
                    else:
                        # a>b
                        for i in range(N-1):
                            if i < b-1:
                                e[i]=c
                            elif i >= b-1 and i < a-1:
                                e[i]=1-c
                            else:
                                e[i]=c
                # compute v_i = (e_{i-1}+e_i + s_i + t_i)/2
                v = []
                for i in range(1,N+1):
                    e_left = e[i-2] if i-2 >=0 else 0
                    e_right = e[i-1] if i-1 < N-1 else 0
                    s = 1 if i==a else 0
                    t = 1 if i==b else 0
                    vi = (e_left + e_right + s + t)//2
                    # ensure integer division exact
                    if (e_left + e_right + s + t) % 2 !=0:
                        # invalid parity (should be even)
                        vi = None
                    v.append(vi)
                # check parity condition
                ok=True
                for i,vi in enumerate(v):
                    if vi is None:
                        ok=False
                        break
                    if vi%2 != p[i]:
                        ok=False
                        break
                if not ok:
                    continue
                moves = sum(v)
                if best is None or moves < best:
                    best = moves
                    best_info = (a,b,c,e,v)
    return best, best_info

[CODE] (turn 2):
from collections import deque

def min_moves_bruteforce(N):
    # initial pattern: tails at odd positions (1-indexed)
    init = tuple(1 if i%2==1 else 0 for i in range(1,N+1))  # 1 = tail, 0 = head? Actually tails-up = need flip to become heads.
    # goal all heads (0)
    goal = tuple(0 for _ in range(N))
    # BFS state: (position index (0..N-1), config tuple)
    # first move can be any coin, so we can start by flipping any coin; that counts as move 1, position = that coin.
    # We'll treat start as after first flip.
    visited=set()
    q=deque()
    for i in range(N):
        # flip coin i
        config = list(init)
        config[i] ^= 1  # toggle
        state = (i, tuple(config))
        visited.add(state)
        q.append((state,1))
        if tuple(config)==goal:
            return 1
    while q:
        (pos, cfg), steps = q.popleft()
        # next moves: flip adjacent coin (pos-1 or pos+1)
        for np in [pos-1, pos+1]:
            if 0 <= np < N:
                newcfg = list(cfg)
                newcfg[np] ^= 1
                newstate = (np, tuple(newcfg))
                if newstate not in visited:
                    if tuple(newcfg)==goal:
                        return steps+1
                    visited.add(newstate)
                    q.append((newstate, steps+1))
    return None

for N in range(1,11):
    print(N, min_moves_bruteforce(N))

[CODE] (turn 2):
from collections import deque

def min_moves_bruteforce(N):
    # initial pattern: tails at odd positions (1-indexed)
    init = tuple(1 if i%2==1 else 0 for i in range(1,N+1))  # 1 = tail, 0 = head? Actually tails-up = need flip to become heads.
    # goal all heads (0)
    goal = tuple(0 for _ in range(N))
    # BFS state: (position index (0..N-1), config tuple)
    # first move can be any coin, so we can start by flipping any coin; that counts as move 1, position = that coin.
    # We'll treat start as after first flip.
    visited=set()
    q=deque()
    for i in range(N):
        # flip coin i
        config = list(init)
        config[i] ^= 1  # toggle
        state = (i, tuple(config))
        visited.add(state)
        q.append((state,1))
        if tuple(config)==goal:
            return 1
    while q:
        (pos, cfg), steps = q.popleft()
        # next moves: flip adjacent coin (pos-1 or pos+1)
        for np in [pos-1, pos+1]:
            if 0 <= np < N:
                newcfg = list(cfg)
                newcfg[np] ^= 1
                newstate = (np, tuple(newcfg))
                if newstate not in visited:
                    if tuple(newcfg)==goal:
                        return steps+1
                    visited.add(newstate)
                    q.append((newstate, steps+1))
    return None

for N in range(1,11):
    print(N, min_moves_bruteforce(N))

def compute_seq(limit):
    seq={}
    for N in range(1,limit+1):
        seq[N]=min_moves_bruteforce(N)
    return seq

from collections import deque

def min_moves_bruteforce(N, limit=1000000):
    init = tuple(1 if i%2==1 else 0 for i in range(1,N+1))
    goal = tuple(0 for _ in range(N))
    visited=set()
    q=deque()
    for i in range(N):
        cfg = list(init)
        cfg[i] ^= 1
        state=(i, tuple(cfg))
        visited.add(state)
        q.append((state,1))
        if tuple(cfg)==goal:
            return 1
    while q:
        (pos,cfg),steps = q.popleft()
        for np in (pos-1,pos+1):
            if 0<=np<N:
                newcfg=list(cfg)
                newcfg[np]^=1
                newstate=(np, tuple(newcfg))
                if newstate not in visited:
                    if tuple(newcfg)==goal:
                        return steps+1
                    visited.add(newstate)
                    q.append((newstate, steps+1))
        if steps>limit:
            break
    return None

for N in range(14,16):
    print(N, min_moves_bruteforce(N))

def min_moves_bruteforce_limit(N, max_states=5_000_000):
    init = tuple(1 if i%2==1 else 0 for i in range(1,N+1))
    goal = tuple(0 for _ in range(N))
    from collections import deque
    visited=set()
    q=deque()
    for i in range(N):
        cfg = list(init)
        cfg[i] ^= 1
        state = (i, tuple(cfg))
        visited.add(state)
        q.append((state,1))
        if tuple(cfg)==goal:
            return 1
    while q:
        (pos,cfg),steps = q.popleft()
        for np in (pos-1,pos+1):
            if 0<=np<N:
                newcfg = list(cfg)
                newcfg[np] ^= 1
                newstate = (np, tuple(newcfg))
                if newstate not in visited:
                    if tuple(newcfg)==goal:
                        return steps+1
                    visited.add(newstate)
                    q.append((newstate, steps+1))
        if len(visited)>max_states:
            return None
    return None

for N in range(17,20,2):
    print(N, min_moves_bruteforce_limit(N, max_states=2_000_000))

[CODE] (turn 12):
def min_moves_dp(N):
    p = [1 if i%2==1 else 0 for i in range(1,N+1)]  # odd nodes need odd visits
    best = None
    best_pair = None
    for a in range(1,N+1):
        for b in range(1,N+1):
            # DP: dict mapping left edge value to min cost
            dp = {0:0}  # before node1, e0=0
            for i in range(1,N+1):
                newdp = {}
                s = 1 if i==a else 0
                t = 1 if i==b else 0
                target_mod = (2 * p[i-1]) % 4
                for L,cost in dp.items():
                    for R in range(4):
                        if (L + R + s + t) % 4 == target_mod:
                            newcost = cost + R
                            # keep minimal cost for state R
                            if R not in newdp or newcost < newdp[R]:
                                newdp[R] = newcost
                dp = newdp
                if not dp:
                    break
            else:
                # after loop, need e_N = 0
                if 0 in dp:
                    total_edges = dp[0]
                    moves = total_edges + 1
                    if best is None or moves < best:
                        best = moves
                        best_pair = (a,b)
    return best, best_pair

for N in range(1,21,2):
    best, pair = min_moves_dp(N)
    print(N, best, pair)

[CODE] (turn 13):
def min_moves_dp_solution(N):
    p = [1 if i%2==1 else 0 for i in range(1,N+1)]
    best = None
    best_pair = None
    best_e = None
    for a in range(1,N+1):
        for b in range(1,N+1):
            dp = {0: (0, [])}  # map left edge value -> (cost, list of e's so far)
            for i in range(1,N+1):
                newdp = {}
                s = 1 if i==a else 0
                t = 1 if i==b else 0
                target_mod = (2 * p[i-1]) % 4
                for L, (cost, seq) in dp.items():
                    for R in range(4):
                        if (L + R + s + t) % 4 == target_mod:
                            newcost = cost + R
                            newseq = seq + [R]
                            if R not in newdp or newcost < newdp[R][0]:
                                newdp[R] = (newcost, newseq)
                dp = newdp
                if not dp:
                    break
            else:
                if 0 in dp:
                    total_edges, seq = dp[0]
                    moves = total_edges + 1
                    if best is None or moves < best:
                        best = moves
                        best_pair = (a,b)
                        best_e = seq
    return best, best_pair, best_e

print(min_moves_dp_solution(5))

def min_moves_dp_solution(N):
    p = [1 if i%2==1 else 0 for i in range(1,N+1)]
    best = None
    best_pair = None
    best_e = None
    for a in range(1,N+1):
        for b in range(1,N+1):
            dp = {0: (0, [])}  # map left edge value -> (cost, list of e's so far)
            for i in range(1,N+1):
                newdp = {}
                s = 1 if i==a else 0
                t = 1 if i==b else 0
                target_mod = (2 * p[i-1]) % 4
                for L, (cost, seq) in dp.items():
                    for R in range(4):
                        if (L + R + s + t) % 4 == target_mod:
                            newcost = cost + R
                            newseq = seq + [R]
                            if R not in newdp or newcost < newdp[R][0]:
                                newdp[R] = (newcost, newseq)
                dp = newdp
                if not dp:
                    break
            else:
                if 0 in dp:
                    total_edges, seq = dp[0]
                    moves = total_edges + 1
                    if best is None or moves < best:
                        best = moves
                        best_pair = (a,b)
                        best_e = seq
    return best, best_pair, best_e

[CODE] (turn 14):
def compute_v(N, a,b, e):
    # e list length N (including e_N=0)
    v=[]
    for i in range(1,N+1):
        e_left = e[i-2] if i-2>=0 else 0
        e_right = e[i-1] if i-1 < len(e) else 0
        s = 1 if i==a else 0
        t = 1 if i==b else 0
        vi = (e_left+e_right + s + t)//2
        v.append(vi)
    return v

e_seq=[0,0,2,2,0]
print(compute_v(5,1,1,e_seq))

def compute_v(N, a,b, e):
    # e list length N (including e_N=0)
    v=[]
    for i in range(1,N+1):
        e_left = e[i-2] if i-2>=0 else 0
        e_right = e[i-1] if i-1 < len(e) else 0
        s = 1 if i==a else 0
        t = 1 if i==b else 0
        vi = (e_left+e_right + s + t)//2
        v.append(vi)
    return v

[CODE] (turn 15):
def min_moves_dp_bound(N):
    p = [1 if i%2==1 else 0 for i in range(1,N+1)]
    best = None
    best_pair = None
    for a in range(1,N+1):
        for b in range(1,N+1):
            dp = {0:0}  # left edge value -> cost sum so far
            for i in range(1,N+1):
                newdp = {}
                s = 1 if i==a else 0
                t = 1 if i==b else 0
                target_mod = (2 * p[i-1]) % 4
                for L,cost in dp.items():
                    # allowed R values:
                    if i == N:
                        R_vals = [0]  # e_N must be 0
                    else:
                        R_vals = [1,2,3]  # at least 1, up to 3 (since we restrict to 0..3)
                    for R in R_vals:
                        if (L + R + s + t) % 4 == target_mod:
                            newcost = cost + R
                            if R not in newdp or newcost < newdp[R]:
                                newdp[R] = newcost
                dp = newdp
                if not dp:
                    break
            else:
                if 0 in dp:
                    total_edges = dp[0]
                    moves = total_edges + 1
                    if best is None or moves < best:
                        best = moves
                        best_pair = (a,b)
    return best, best_pair

for N in range(1,21,2):
    best, pair = min_moves_dp_bound(N)
    print(N, best, pair)

[CODE] (turn 15):

[CODE] (turn 16):
def min_moves_dp_bound_fast(N):
    # Since optimal start and end seem to alternate: start at 1, end at N-1? Observed pattern:
    # For N ≡ 1 mod 4, best pair (2, N-1) maybe? Actually from results:
    # N=5 (pair 2,4), N=9 (2,8), N=13 (2,12), N=17 (2,16) -> start=2, end=N-1
    # For N ≡ 3 mod 4, pair (1,N-1) e.g., N=7 (1,6), N=11 (1,10), N=15 (1,14), N=19 (1,18)
    # So pattern holds.
    # We'll compute moves using DP only for those pairs to verify formula.
    p = [1 if i%2==1 else 0 for i in range(1,N+1)]
    best = None
    best_pair = None
    # Determine candidate pairs based on N mod 4
    if N % 4 == 1:
        candidates = [(2, N-1)]
    else:
        candidates = [(1, N-1)]
    for a,b in candidates:
        dp = {0:0}
        for i in range(1,N+1):
            newdp = {}
            s = 1 if i==a else 0
            t = 1 if i==b else 0
            target_mod = (2 * p[i-1]) % 4
            for L,cost in dp.items():
                if i == N:
                    R_vals = [0]
                else:
                    R_vals = [1,2,3]
                for R in R_vals:
                    if (L + R + s + t) % 4 == target_mod:
                        newcost = cost + R
                        if R not in newdp or newcost < newdp[R]:
                            newdp[R] = newcost
            dp = newdp
            if not dp:
                break
        else:
            if 0 in dp:
                moves = dp[0] + 1
                best = moves
                best_pair = (a,b)
    return best, best_pair

for N in range(3,41,2):
    best,_ = min_moves_dp_bound_fast(N)
    formula = (2*N - 2) if N%4==3 else (2*N - 3)
    print(N, best, formula, best==formula)

[CODE] (turn 16):
def min_moves_dp_bound_fast(N):
    # Since optimal start and end seem to alternate: start at 1, end at N-1? Observed pattern:
    # For N ≡ 1 mod 4, best pair (2, N-1) maybe? Actually from results:
    # N=5 (pair 2,4), N=9 (2,8), N=13 (2,12), N=17 (2,16) -> start=2, end=N-1
    # For N ≡ 3 mod 4, pair (1,N-1) e.g., N=7 (1,6), N=11 (1,10), N=15 (1,14), N=19 (1,18)
    # So pattern holds.
    # We'll compute moves using DP only for those pairs to verify formula.
    p = [1 if i%2==1 else 0 for i in range(1,N+1)]
    best = None
    best_pair = None
    # Determine candidate pairs based on N mod 4
    if N % 4 == 1:
        candidates = [(2, N-1)]
    else:
        candidates = [(1, N-1)]
    for a,b in candidates:
        dp = {0:0}
        for i in range(1,N+1):
            newdp = {}
            s = 1 if i==a else 0
            t = 1 if i==b else 0
            target_mod = (2 * p[i-1]) % 4
            for L,cost in dp.items():
                if i == N:
                    R_vals = [0]
                else:
                    R_vals = [1,2,3]
                for R in R_vals:
                    if (L + R + s + t) % 4 == target_mod:
                        newcost = cost + R
                        if R not in newdp or newcost < newdp[R]:
                            newdp[R] = newcost
            dp = newdp
            if not dp:
                break
        else:
            if 0 in dp:
                moves = dp[0] + 1
                best = moves
                best_pair = (a,b)
    return best, best_pair

for N in range(3,41,2):
    best,_ = min_moves_dp_bound_fast(N)
    formula = (2*N - 2) if N%4==3 else (2*N - 3)
    print(N, best, formula, best==formula)

[CODE] (turn 1):
from collections import deque

def min_moves(n):
    # initial state: alternating T (0) and H (1) maybe, with leftmost T.
    # Represent as bitmask: 0 for T, 1 for H.
    init = 0
    for i in range(n):
        if i%2==0: # index 0 corresponds to position 1 (odd) tails
            init |= 0<<i
        else:
            init |= 1<<i
    # target all heads: all bits 1
    target = (1<<n)-1
    # BFS over states with move constraints: need to keep track of previous flipped position.
    # State: (mask, prev_pos) where prev_pos is index of last flipped coin (0..n-1) or -1 for start (no previous).
    # On first move, can flip any coin.
    start_state = (init, -1)
    q = deque()
    q.append(start_state)
    dist = {start_state:0}
    while q:
        mask, prev = q.popleft()
        d = dist[(mask, prev)]
        if mask == target:
            return d
        # possible flips:
        if prev == -1:
            candidates = range(n)
        else:
            candidates = []
            if prev-1 >=0:
                candidates.append(prev-1)
            if prev+1 < n:
                candidates.append(prev+1)
        for pos in candidates:
            newmask = mask ^ (1<<pos)  # flip
            newstate = (newmask, pos)
            if newstate not in dist:
                dist[newstate] = d+1
                q.append(newstate)
    return None

for n in range(1,13):
    print(n, min_moves(n))

def min_moves_limit(n):
    init = 0
    for i in range(n):
        if i%2==0:
            init |= 0<<i
        else:
            init |= 1<<i
    target = (1<<n)-1
    from collections import deque
    start = (init, -1)
    q=deque([start])
    dist={start:0}
    while q:
        mask, prev = q.popleft()
        d=dist[(mask,prev)]
        if mask==target:
            return d
        if prev==-1:
            cand=range(n)
        else:
            cand=[]
            if prev-1>=0: cand.append(prev-1)
            if prev+1<n: cand.append(prev+1)
        for pos in cand:
            newmask=mask ^ (1<<pos)
            ns=(newmask,pos)
            if ns not in dist:
                dist[ns]=d+1
                q.append(ns)
    return None

def min_path(n):
    init = 0
    for i in range(n):
        if i%2==0:
            init |= 0<<i
        else:
            init |= 1<<i
    target = (1<<n)-1
    from collections import deque
    start = (init, -1)
    q=deque([start])
    parent={start: None}
    while q:
        mask, prev = q.popleft()
        d = 0 # not needed
        if mask==target:
            # reconstruct path
            path=[]
            cur=(mask,prev)
            while cur is not None:
                path.append(cur)
                cur=parent[cur]
            path.reverse()
            return path
        if prev==-1:
            cand=range(n)
        else:
            cand=[]
            if prev-1>=0: cand.append(prev-1)
            if prev+1<n: cand.append(prev+1)
        for pos in cand:
            newmask=mask ^ (1<<pos)
            ns=(newmask,pos)
            if ns not in parent:
                parent[ns]=(mask,prev)
                q.append(ns)
    return None

path5=min_path(5)
len(path5)-1  # moves count

def min_path(n):
    init = 0
    for i in range(n):
        if i%2==0:
            init |= 0<<i
        else:
            init |= 1<<i
    target = (1<<n)-1
    from collections import deque
    start = (init, -1)
    q=deque([start])
    parent={start: None}
    while q:
        mask, prev = q.popleft()
        d = 0 # not needed
        if mask==target:
            # reconstruct path
            path=[]
            cur=(mask,prev)
            while cur is not None:
                path.append(cur)
                cur=parent[cur]
            path.reverse()
            return path
        if prev==-1:
            cand=range(n)
        else:
            cand=[]
            if prev-1>=0: cand.append(prev-1)
            if prev+1<n: cand.append(prev+1)
        for pos in cand:
            newmask=mask ^ (1<<pos)
            ns=(newmask,pos)
            if ns not in parent:
                parent[ns]=(mask,prev)
                q.append(ns)
    return None

[CODE] (turn 1):
from collections import deque

def min_moves(N):
    # N odd
    # initial config bits: 1 for T (odd positions), 0 for H (even)
    init = tuple(1 if i%2==0 else 0 for i in range(N))  # i 0-index: position 1 => i=0 odd => T=1
    goal = tuple([0]*N)
    # BFS
    # state: (pos, config) where pos is index of last flipped coin
    # distance = number of moves so far
    visited = set()
    q = deque()
    # first move any coin
    for p in range(N):
        # flip p
        config = list(init)
        config[p] ^= 1
        state = (p, tuple(config))
        visited.add(state)
        q.append((state, 1))
    while q:
        (pos, config), d = q.popleft()
        if config == goal:
            return d
        # next moves: adjacent positions
        for np in (pos-1, pos+1):
            if 0 <= np < N:
                newconfig = list(config)
                newconfig[np] ^= 1
                newstate = (np, tuple(newconfig))
                if newstate not in visited:
                    visited.add(newstate)
                    q.append((newstate, d+1))
    return None

for N in [1,3,5,7,9,11,13]:
    print(N, min_moves(N))

def min_moves(N):

    # initial config bits: 1 for T (odd positions), 0 for H (even)

    # distance = number of moves so far

    for p in range(N):
        # flip p
        config = list(init)
        config[p] ^= 1
        state = (p, tuple(config))
        visited.add(state)
        q.append((state, 1))
    while q:

                newconfig[np] ^= 1

                    visited.add(newstate)

def min_moves_limit(N, limit=1000000):
    init = tuple(1 if i%2==0 else 0 for i in range(N))
    goal = tuple([0]*N)
    from collections import deque
    visited = set()
    q = deque()
    for p in range(N):
        config = list(init)
        config[p] ^= 1
        state = (p, tuple(config))
        visited.add(state)
        q.append((state, 1))
    while q:
        (pos, config), d = q.popleft()
        if config == goal:
            return d
        for np in (pos-1, pos+1):
            if 0 <= np < N:
                newconfig = list(config)
                newconfig[np] ^= 1
                newstate = (np, tuple(newconfig))
                if newstate not in visited:
                    visited.add(newstate)
                    q.append((newstate, d+1))
        if len(visited) > limit:
            return None
    return None

for N in range(1,19,2):
    print(N, min_moves_limit(N, limit=500000))

def min_moves_limit(N, limit=1000000):
    init = tuple(1 if i%2==0 else 0 for i in range(N))
    goal = tuple([0]*N)
    from collections import deque
    visited = set()
    q = deque()
    for p in range(N):
        config = list(init)
        config[p] ^= 1
        state = (p, tuple(config))
        visited.add(state)
        q.append((state, 1))
    while q:
        (pos, config), d = q.popleft()
        if config == goal:
            return d
        for np in (pos-1, pos+1):
            if 0 <= np < N:
                newconfig = list(config)
                newconfig[np] ^= 1
                newstate = (np, tuple(newconfig))
                if newstate not in visited:
                    visited.add(newstate)
                    q.append((newstate, d+1))
        if len(visited) > limit:
            return None
    return None

from scipy.optimize import milp, LinearConstraint, Bounds
import numpy as np, math, itertools, sys, warnings, traceback, os, json, pprint, copy, collections, fractions, random, time, re, string, hashlib, textwrap, inspect, decimal, statistics, heapq, math, numpy as np

[OUTPUT] (turn 6):

[CODE] (turn 7):
def min_moves_milp(N):
    # Variables:
    # a_i for i=0..N-2: traversals from i to i+1
    # b_i for i=0..N-2: traversals from i+1 to i
    # start_i binary for each vertex i (0..N-1)
    # end_i binary for each vertex i
    # k_i integer >=0 for parity constraint
    # Also we need flow constraints.
    n_edges = N-1
    # variable ordering: a0..a_{n_edges-1}, b0..b_{n_edges-1}, start0..start_{N-1}, end0..end_{N-1}, k0..k_{N-1}
    total_vars = 2*n_edges + 2*N + N
    # We'll use c vector zero (objective minimize sum a+b)
    c = np.zeros(total_vars)
    # set objective coefficients for a and b to 1
    c[:n_edges] = 1  # a
    c[n_edges:2*n_edges] = 1  # b
    # bounds: a,b >=0 integer; start,end binary (0/1); k >=0 integer
    lb = np.zeros(total_vars)
    ub = np.full(total_vars, np.inf)
    # set integer indices
    integrality = np.zeros(total_vars, dtype=int)
    # a,b integer
    integrality[:2*n_edges] = 1
    # start,end binary
    integrality[2*n_edges:2*n_edges+2*N] = 1
    # k integer
    integrality[2*n_edges+2*N:] = 1
    # set upper bounds for binary variables to 1
    ub[2*n_edges:2*n_edges+2*N] = 1
    
    # Constraints:
    # 1) Exactly one start and one end (could be same)
    A_eq = []
    b_eq = []
    # sum start_i = 1
    row = np.zeros(total_vars)
    row[2*n_edges:2*n_edges+N] = 1
    A_eq.append(row)
    b_eq.append(1)
    # sum end_i = 1
    row = np.zeros(total_vars)
    row[2*n_edges+N:2*n_edges+2*N] = 1
    A_eq.append(row)
    b_eq.append(1)
    
    # 2) Flow constraints for each vertex i: out - in = start_i - end_i
    # out_i = sum of traversals leaving i:
    # For i=0: out = a0
    # For i=N-1: out = b_{N-2}
    # For interior i: out = a_i + b_{i-1}
    # in_i = sum of traversals entering i:
    # For i=0: in = b0
    # For i=N-1: in = a_{N-2}
    # For interior i: in = b_i + a_{i-1}
    for i in range(N):
        row = np.zeros(total_vars)
        # out term
        if i == 0:
            row[0] = 1  # a0
        elif i == N-1:
            row[n_edges + (N-2)] = 1  # b_{N-2}
        else:
            row[i] = 1  # a_i
            row[n_edges + (i-1)] = 1  # b_{i-1}
        # in term subtract
        if i == 0:
            row[n_edges + 0] -= 1  # -b0
        elif i == N-1:
            row[N-2] -= 1  # -a_{N-2}
        else:
            row[n_edges + i] -= 1  # -b_i
            row[i-1] -= 1  # -a_{i-1}
        # start_i - end_i term
        row[2*n_edges + i] += 1   # + start_i
        row[2*n_edges + N + i] -= 1  # - end_i
        A_eq.append(row)
        b_eq.append(0)
    
    # 3) Parity constraints: in_i - start_i = 2 * k_i
    # i.e., in_i - start_i - 2*k_i = 0
    for i in range(N):
        row = np.zeros(total_vars)
        # in_i coefficient
        if i == 0:
            row[n_edges + 0] = 1  # b0
        elif i == N-1:
            row[N-2] = 1  # a_{N-2}
        else:
            row[n_edges + i] = 1  # b_i
            row[i-1] = 1  # a_{i-1}
        # - start_i
        row[2*n_edges + i] -= 1
        # -2*k_i
        row[2*n_edges + 2*N + i] = -2
        A_eq.append(row)
        b_eq.append(0)
    
    A_eq = np.array(A_eq)
    b_eq = np.array(b_eq)
    
    # No inequality constraints
    constraints = LinearConstraint(A_eq, b_eq, b_eq)
    bounds = Bounds(lb, ub)
    # Solve MILP
    res = milp(c=c, constraints=constraints, bounds=bounds, integrality=integrality, options={'presolve': True, 'disp': False})
    if not res.success:
        print("MILP failed", res.message)
        return None
    total_traversals = sum(res.x[:2*n_edges])
    total_moves = int(total_traversals) + 1
    return total_moves

for N in [3,5,7,9,11,13,15]:
    print(N, min_moves_milp(N))

[CODE] (turn 7):
def min_moves_milp(N):
    # Variables:
    # a_i for i=0..N-2: traversals from i to i+1
    # b_i for i=0..N-2: traversals from i+1 to i
    # start_i binary for each vertex i (0..N-1)
    # end_i binary for each vertex i
    # k_i integer >=0 for parity constraint
    # Also we need flow constraints.
    n_edges = N-1
    # variable ordering: a0..a_{n_edges-1}, b0..b_{n_edges-1}, start0..start_{N-1}, end0..end_{N-1}, k0..k_{N-1}
    total_vars = 2*n_edges + 2*N + N
    # We'll use c vector zero (objective minimize sum a+b)
    c = np.zeros(total_vars)

    c[:n_edges] = 1  # a
    c[n_edges:2*n_edges] = 1  # b
    # bounds: a,b >=0 integer; start,end binary (0/1); k >=0 integer
    lb = np.zeros(total_vars)
    ub = np.full(total_vars, np.inf)
    # set integer indices
    integrality = np.zeros(total_vars, dtype=int)
    # a,b integer
    integrality[:2*n_edges] = 1
    # start,end binary
    integrality[2*n_edges:2*n_edges+2*N] = 1
    # k integer
    integrality[2*n_edges+2*N:] = 1
    # set upper bounds for binary variables to 1
    ub[2*n_edges:2*n_edges+2*N] = 1
    
    # Constraints:
    # 1) Exactly one start and one end (could be same)
    A_eq = []
    b_eq = []
    # sum start_i = 1
    row = np.zeros(total_vars)
    row[2*n_edges:2*n_edges+N] = 1
    A_eq.append(row)
    b_eq.append(1)
    # sum end_i = 1
    row = np.zeros(total_vars)
    row[2*n_edges+N:2*n_edges+2*N] = 1
    A_eq.append(row)
    b_eq.append(1)
    
    # 2) Flow constraints for each vertex i: out - in = start_i - end_i
    # out_i = sum of traversals leaving i:
    # For i=0: out = a0
    # For i=N-1: out = b_{N-2}
    # For interior i: out = a_i + b_{i-1}
    # in_i = sum of traversals entering i:
    # For i=0: in = b0
    # For i=N-1: in = a_{N-2}
    # For interior i: in = b_i + a_{i-1}
    for i in range(N):
        row = np.zeros(total_vars)
        # out term
        if i == 0:
            row[0] = 1  # a0
        elif i == N-1:
            row[n_edges + (N-2)] = 1  # b_{N-2}
        else:
            row[i] = 1  # a_i
            row[n_edges + (i-1)] = 1  # b_{i-1}
        # in term subtract
        if i == 0:
            row[n_edges + 0] -= 1  # -b0
        elif i == N-1:
            row[N-2] -= 1  # -a_{N-2}
        else:
            row[n_edges + i] -= 1  # -b_i
            row[i-1] -= 1  # -a_{i-1}
        # start_i - end_i term
        row[2*n_edges + i] += 1   # + start_i
        row[2*n_edges + N + i] -= 1  # - end_i
        A_eq.append(row)
        b_eq.append(0)
    
    # 3) Parity constraints: in_i - start_i = 2 * k_i
    # i.e., in_i - start_i - 2*k_i = 0
    for i in range(N):
        row = np.zeros(total_vars)
        # in_i coefficient
        if i == 0:
            row[n_edges + 0] = 1  # b0
        elif i == N-1:
            row[N-2] = 1  # a_{N-2}
        else:
            row[n_edges + i] = 1  # b_i
            row[i-1] = 1  # a_{i-1}
        # - start_i
        row[2*n_edges + i] -= 1
        # -2*k_i
        row[2*n_edges + 2*N + i] = -2
        A_eq.append(row)
        b_eq.append(0)
    
    A_eq = np.array(A_eq)
    b_eq = np.array(b_eq)
    
    # No inequality constraints
    constraints = LinearConstraint(A_eq, b_eq, b_eq)
    bounds = Bounds(lb, ub)
    # Solve MILP
    res = milp(c=c, constraints=constraints, bounds=bounds, integrality=integrality, options={'presolve': True, 'disp': False})
    if not res.success:
        print("MILP failed", res.message)
        return None
    total_traversals = sum(res.x[:2*n_edges])
    total_moves = int(total_traversals) + 1
    return total_moves

for N in [3,5,7,9,11,13,15]:
    print(N, min_moves_milp(N))

[CODE] (turn 8):
def min_moves_milp(N):
    n_edges = N-1
    total_vars = 2*n_edges + 2*N + N  # a,b,start,end,k
    c = np.zeros(total_vars)
    c[:n_edges] = 1
    c[n_edges:2*n_edges] = 1
    # bounds
    lb = np.zeros(total_vars)
    ub = np.full(total_vars, np.inf)
    integrality = np.zeros(total_vars, dtype=int)
    integrality[:2*n_edges] = 1
    integrality[2*n_edges:2*n_edges+2*N] = 1
    integrality[2*n_edges+2*N:] = 1
    ub[2*n_edges:2*n_edges+2*N] = 1  # binary
    
    # constraints
    rows = []
    rhs = []
    # exactly one start
    row = np.zeros(total_vars)
    row[2*n_edges:2*n_edges+N] = 1
    rows.append(row); rhs.append(1)
    # exactly one end
    row = np.zeros(total_vars)
    row[2*n_edges+N:2*n_edges+2*N] = 1
    rows.append(row); rhs.append(1)
    
    # flow constraints
    for i in range(N):
        row = np.zeros(total_vars)
        # out
        if i == 0:
            row[0] = 1  # a0
        elif i == N-1:
            row[n_edges + (N-2)] = 1  # b_{N-2}
        else:
            row[i] = 1  # a_i
            row[n_edges + (i-1)] = 1  # b_{i-1}
        # in (subtract)
        if i == 0:
            row[n_edges + 0] -= 1  # -b0
        elif i == N-1:
            row[N-2] -= 1  # -a_{N-2}
        else:
            row[n_edges + i] -= 1  # -b_i
            row[i-1] -= 1  # -a_{i-1}
        # start - end
        row[2*n_edges + i] += 1
        row[2*n_edges + N + i] -= 1
        rows.append(row); rhs.append(0)
    
    # parity constraints: in_i + start_i - p_i = 2*k_i
    for i in range(N):
        row = np.zeros(total_vars)
        # in_i coefficient
        if i == 0:
            row[n_edges + 0] = 1  # b0
        elif i == N-1:
            row[N-2] = 1  # a_{N-2}
        else:
            row[n_edges + i] = 1  # b_i
            row[i-1] = 1  # a_{i-1}
        # + start_i
        row[2*n_edges + i] += 1
        # - 2*k_i
        row[2*n_edges + 2*N + i] = -2
        # RHS = p_i
        p_i = 1 if (i % 2 == 0) else 0  # odd positions (i=0) have T=1
        rows.append(row); rhs.append(p_i)
    
    A_eq = np.array(rows)
    b_eq = np.array(rhs)
    constraints = LinearConstraint(A_eq, b_eq, b_eq)
    bounds = Bounds(lb, ub)
    res = milp(c=c, constraints=constraints, bounds=bounds, integrality=integrality, options={'presolve': True, 'disp': False})
    if not res.success:
        print("MILP failed", res.message)
        return None
    total_traversals = sum(res.x[:2*n_edges])
    total_moves = int(round(total_traversals)) + 1
    return total_moves

for N in [3,5,7,9,11,13,15]:
    print(N, min_moves_milp(N))

[CODE] (turn 9):
from collections import deque

def min_moves_bruteforce(N, max_depth=10):
    init = tuple(1 if i%2==0 else 0 for i in range(N))
    goal = tuple([0]*N)
    # BFS
    visited = set()
    q = deque()
    # first move any coin
    for p in range(N):
        config = list(init)
        config[p] ^= 1
        state = (p, tuple(config))
        visited.add(state)
        q.append((state, 1))
    while q:
        (pos, config), d = q.popleft()
        if config == goal:
            return d
        if d >= max_depth:
            continue
        for np in (pos-1, pos+1):
            if 0 <= np < N:
                newconfig = list(config)
                newconfig[np] ^= 1
                newstate = (np, tuple(newconfig))
                if newstate not in visited:
                    visited.add(newstate)
                    q.append((newstate, d+1))
    return None

for N in [3,5,7,9]:
    for depth in range(1,9):
        m = min_moves_bruteforce(N, max_depth=depth)
        if m is not None:
            print(N, "found", m, "within depth", depth)
            break

from collections import deque

def min_moves_bruteforce(N, max_depth=10):
    init = tuple(1 if i%2==0 else 0 for i in range(N))
    goal = tuple([0]*N)
    # BFS
    visited = set()
    q = deque()
    # first move any coin
    for p in range(N):
        config = list(init)
        config[p] ^= 1
        state = (p, tuple(config))
        visited.add(state)
        q.append((state, 1))
    while q:
        (pos, config), d = q.popleft()
        if config == goal:
            return d
        if d >= max_depth:
            continue
        for np in (pos-1, pos+1):
            if 0 <= np < N:
                newconfig = list(config)
                newconfig[np] ^= 1
                newstate = (np, tuple(newconfig))
                if newstate not in visited:
                    visited.add(newstate)
                    q.append((newstate, d+1))
    return None

for N in [3,5,7,9]:
    for depth in range(1,9):
        m = min_moves_bruteforce(N, max_depth=depth)
        if m is not None:
            print(N, "found", m, "within depth", depth)
            break

[CODE] (turn 10):
def solve_milp(N):
    n_edges = N-1
    total_vars = 2*n_edges + 2*N + N
    c = np.zeros(total_vars)
    c[:n_edges] = 1
    c[n_edges:2*n_edges] = 1
    lb = np.zeros(total_vars)
    ub = np.full(total_vars, np.inf)
    integrality = np.zeros(total_vars, dtype=int)
    integrality[:2*n_edges] = 1
    integrality[2*n_edges:2*n_edges+2*N] = 1
    integrality[2*n_edges+2*N:] = 1
    ub[2*n_edges:2*n_edges+2*N] = 1
    rows = []
    rhs = []
    # start sum =1
    row = np.zeros(total_vars); row[2*n_edges:2*n_edges+N]=1; rows.append(row); rhs.append(1)
    # end sum=1
    row = np.zeros(total_vars); row[2*n_edges+N:2*n_edges+2*N]=1; rows.append(row); rhs.append(1)
    # flow
    for i in range(N):
        row = np.zeros(total_vars)
        if i == 0:
            row[0] = 1
        elif i == N-1:
            row[n_edges + (N-2)] = 1
        else:
            row[i] = 1
            row[n_edges + (i-1)] = 1
        # in subtract
        if i == 0:
            row[n_edges + 0] -= 1
        elif i == N-1:
            row[N-2] -= 1
        else:
            row[n_edges + i] -= 1
            row[i-1] -= 1
        # start - end
        row[2*n_edges + i] += 1
        row[2*n_edges + N + i] -= 1
        rows.append(row); rhs.append(0)
    # parity: in_i + start_i - p_i = 2*k_i
    for i in range(N):
        row = np.zeros(total_vars)
        if i == 0:
            row[n_edges + 0] = 1
        elif i == N-1:
            row[N-2] = 1
        else:
            row[n_edges + i] = 1
            row[i-1] = 1
        row[2*n_edges + i] += 1
        row[2*n_edges + 2*N + i] = -2
        p_i = 1 if i%2==0 else 0
        rows.append(row); rhs.append(p_i)
    A_eq = np.array(rows)
    b_eq = np.array(rhs)
    constraints = LinearConstraint(A_eq, b_eq, b_eq)
    bounds = Bounds(lb, ub)
    res = milp(c=c, constraints=constraints, bounds=bounds, integrality=integrality, options={'presolve': True, 'disp': False})
    if not res.success:
        print("fail", res.message)
        return None
    # extract solution
    sol = res.x
    a = sol[:n_edges]
    b = sol[n_edges:2*n_edges]
    start = sol[2*n_edges:2*n_edges+N]
    end = sol[2*n_edges+N:2*n_edges+2*N]
    k = sol[2*n_edges+2*N:]
    return dict(a=a,b=b,start=start,end=end,k=k,total_moves=int(round(sum(a)+sum(b)))+1)

sol5 = solve_milp(5)

def solve_milp(N):
    n_edges = N-1
    total_vars = 2*n_edges + 2*N + N
    c = np.zeros(total_vars)

            row[n_edges + i] = 1

        row[2*n_edges + i] += 1

    end = sol[2*n_edges+N:2*n_edges+2*N]
    k = sol[2*n_edges+2*N:]

def find_path(N):
    init = tuple(1 if i%2==0 else 0 for i in range(N))
    goal = tuple([0]*N)
    from collections import deque
    visited = {}
    q = deque()
    for p in range(N):
        config = list(init)
        config[p] ^= 1
        state = (p, tuple(config))
        visited[state] = (None, None)  # parent
        q.append((state, 1))
    while q:
        (pos, config), d = q.popleft()
        if config == goal:
            # reconstruct path
            path = []
            cur = (pos, config)
            while visited[cur][0] is not None:
                prev, move = visited[cur]
                path.append(move)
                cur = prev
            path.append(cur[0])  # initial start coin
            path = list(reversed(path))
            return path, d
        for np in (pos-1, pos+1):
            if 0 <= np < N:
                newconfig = list(config)
                newconfig[np] ^= 1
                newstate = (np, tuple(newconfig))
                if newstate not in visited:
                    visited[newstate] = ((pos, config), np)
                    q.append((newstate, d+1))
    return None

path5, moves5 = find_path(5)
path5, moves5

def find_path(N):

        config[p] ^= 1
        state = (p, tuple(config))
        visited[state] = (None, None)  # parent
        q.append((state, 1))
    while q:
        (pos, config), d = q.popleft()
        if config == goal:
            # reconstruct path
            path = []
            cur = (pos, config)
            while visited[cur][0] is not None:
                prev, move = visited[cur]
                path.append(move)
                cur = prev
            path.append(cur[0])  # initial start coin
            path = list(reversed(path))
            return path, d
        for np in (pos-1, pos+1):

                newconfig[np] ^= 1

[CODE] (turn 14):
def construct_walk(N):
    # assume N odd >=3
    # start at position 2 (index 1)
    seq = [1]  # 0-indexed start at index1 (position 2)
    # move left to position 1 (index0)
    seq.append(0)
    i = 0
    while i+2 < N:
        # currently at odd index i (0-based even? Actually i is odd position index 0)
        # Move right to even i+1
        seq.append(i+1)
        # Move right to odd i+2
        seq.append(i+2)
        # Move left back to even i+1
        seq.append(i+1)
        i += 2
    # after loop, we may be at some position; need to finish at rightmost odd?
    # If we ended at even position (i+1) where i+2 == N-1? Let's handle.
    # After loop, i is last odd visited (maybe N-1 if N odd)
    # At this point, seq ends at even i+1 (since we appended left back). Need to move right to next odd if any.
    # Actually loop condition ensures i+2 < N, i.e., i <= N-3.
    # After loop, i = N-3 (if N>=5). Then we have visited up to i+2 = N-1 (last odd) and then back to i+1 = N-2 (second last even).
    # Need to finally move right to last odd N-1? Wait N-1 is index N-2? Let's compute.
    # Let's just move right to last odd (index N-1) if not already there.
    if seq[-1] != N-1:
        # move right to N-2 (even) then to N-1 (odd)
        # currently at even N-2? Actually seq[-1] is even N-2.
        seq.append(N-1)  # move right to last odd
    return seq

def moves_count(N):
    seq = construct_walk(N)
    return len(seq)

for N in [5,7,9,11,13,15]:
    print(N, moves_count(N))

def construct_walk(N):
    # assume N odd >=3
    # start at position 2 (index 1)
    seq = [1]  # 0-indexed start at index1 (position 2)
    # move left to position 1 (index0)
    seq.append(0)
    i = 0
    while i+2 < N:
        # currently at odd index i (0-based even? Actually i is odd position index 0)
        # Move right to even i+1
        seq.append(i+1)
        # Move right to odd i+2
        seq.append(i+2)
        # Move left back to even i+1
        seq.append(i+1)
        i += 2
    # after loop, we may be at some position; need to finish at rightmost odd?
    # If we ended at even position (i+1) where i+2 == N-1? Let's handle.
    # After loop, i is last odd visited (maybe N-1 if N odd)
    # At this point, seq ends at even i+1 (since we appended left back). Need to move right to next odd if any.
    # Actually loop condition ensures i+2 < N, i.e., i <= N-3.
    # After loop, i = N-3 (if N>=5). Then we have visited up to i+2 = N-1 (last odd) and then back to i+1 = N-2 (second last even).
    # Need to finally move right to last odd N-1? Wait N-1 is index N-2? Let's compute.
    # Let's just move right to last odd (index N-1) if not already there.
    if seq[-1] != N-1:
        # move right to N-2 (even) then to N-1 (odd)
        # currently at even N-2? Actually seq[-1] is even N-2.
        seq.append(N-1)  # move right to last odd
    return seq

def moves_count(N):
    seq = construct_walk(N)
    return len(seq)

    print(N, moves_count(N))

[CODE] (turn 16):
def simulate(seq, N):
    # seq list of positions (0-indexed) visited in order, each visited flips coin
    config = [1 if i%2==0 else 0 for i in range(N)]  # initial
    for pos in seq:
        config[pos] ^= 1
    return config

def is_all_heads(config):
    return all(v==0 for v in config)

for N in [5,7,9,11]:
    seq = construct_walk(N)
    final = simulate(seq, N)
    print(N, len(seq), is_all_heads(final))

[CODE] (turn 16):
def simulate(seq, N):
    # seq list of positions (0-indexed) visited in order, each visited flips coin
    config = [1 if i%2==0 else 0 for i in range(N)]  # initial
    for pos in seq:
        config[pos] ^= 1
    return config

def is_all_heads(config):
    return all(v==0 for v in config)

for N in [5,7,9,11]:
    seq = construct_walk(N)
    final = simulate(seq, N)
    print(N, len(seq), is_all_heads(final))

[CODE] (turn 1):
def walk_counts(n):
    # n vertices labeled 1..n
    seq = list(range(1, n+1)) + list(range(n-1, 1, -1))  # start 1..n then n-1..2
    # This ends at vertex 2
    from collections import Counter
    cnt = Counter(seq)
    return cnt

def walk_counts_variant(n):
    # start at 1, go to n, then back to n-1..1 (full return)
    seq = list(range(1, n+1)) + list(range(n-1, 0, -1))
    from collections import Counter
    cnt = Counter(seq)
    return cnt

for n in [5,6,7]:
    print(n, walk_counts(n))
    print(n, walk_counts_variant(n))

import sympy as sp

def solve_demand(n):
    # variables x_1..x_{n-1}
    xs = sp.symbols('x1:'+str(n))
    equations = []
    # x0 = 0, xn = 0
    for i in range(1, n+1):
        left = xs[i-2] if i-2 >=0 else 0  # x_{i-1}
        right = xs[i-1] if i-1 < n-1 else 0  # x_i
        d = 2 if i%2==1 else 0
        equations.append(sp.Eq(left + right, d))
    sol = sp.solve(equations, xs, dict=True)
    return sol

print(solve_demand(5))
